# Winners matrix, b200

Generated by `tools/winners.py` from the phase 3 batches under `/Users/estehle/Downloads/cccl-topk-swarm/dsb_experiments/topk_swarm/state/results/eval-engineer/phase3/b200`. Nodes: umbriel-b200-047, umbriel-b200-068. Harness: 0.10. Latency in cycles per call, throughput in Gelem/s. Margin over the runner-up and over the reference control measured in the same lock section (latency: how much slower the other is, throughput: how much faster the winner is). Cells with a margin over the runner-up below 15 percent are listed as close and get the tie_heavy pass.

Reading notes. (1) Every value is from the harness version named above; cards made with earlier versions differ by the documented control changes (harness/README.md section 3.1), for example B4_v4 against B3_tuned at fp32 N 16384 reads 0.78 to 0.80 of the card ratio under 0.10. (2) Tables whose input pattern carries `@runsM` are the sorted-runs track (chunk merges on M presorted runs): only adapters measured on that input appear there, a single-candidate row has no runner-up, and these rows are not head-to-head with the random-input tables. (3) A runner-up marked `(spills)` has register spills or local memory at that cell, so its margin is against a degraded form (B2_packed above 32 items per thread is the known case). (4) When a table's cells were measured on more than one node the `node` column says which, candidates of one cell always share a node and a session.
